AUTHORITY: STATE-GUIDANCE

# STC Assessor's Manual Volume II (2025) — S

> Source: michigan.gov STC Assessors-Manual, retrieved 2026-08-03.
> Original PDF at source-pdfs/assessor-manual/stc-assessors-manual-vol2-14s.pdf, text via pdftotext -layout.
> COST SCHEDULES. These are tables. Extraction preserves most row labels and cost
> figures, but ⚠ ALWAYS VERIFY A COST FIGURE AGAINST THE PDF before using it in a
> valuation — a mis-parsed table cell is indistinguishable from a correct one in text.
> Volume I = residential and agricultural. Volume II = commercial and industrial.

                                                         STORES – DEPARTMENT




                          GOOD CLASS C                                                                                  GOOD CLASS B

OCCUPANCY DESCRIPTION: These buildings are often two or                                  INCLUDED IN COSTS: Architects’ fees and contractors’ overhead
more stories designed to display and sell multiple lines of merchan-                     and profit. Allowances are included for suitable office and employee
dise. The front elevations usually vary with the quality of the store.                   areas and restroom facilities. Elevators are included where designat-
The higher-quality department stores have large, ornate display areas                    ed with an asterisk (*).
and fronts, while at the average quality level, the displays are relative-
ly smaller. Most department stores have elevators and escalators to                      NOT INCLUDED IN COSTS: Sprinklers, furnishings, signs or equip-
transport people to the various levels in the building. Floor coverings                  ment.
are a mixture of carpet and resilient tile, with the better qualities utiliz-
ing high traffic type floor finishes such as terrazzo. Department stores

                                                                                           ment.
Most anchor stores have some elevators and escalators. Floor cov-
erings are a mixture of carpet and resilient tile, with the better qual-
ities utilizing high traffic type floor finishes such as terrazzo. Anchor
stores generally have combined heating and cooling systems and
good store lighting. Allowances are included for suitable office and
employee areas and restroom facilities.
